- **firebase-backend-security.pii-logging-platform.knowledge**: - An EXEMPTION inside a sanitizer (the one branch that KEEPS hostile markup) is graded by what it matches, not by what it names. - **firebase-backend-security.reads-cache-offline.knowledge**: - Hydrating a page of parents with a per-parent SUBCOLLECTION (archive: BUT-1832 `message_query_module.dart`) has four failure modes: a per-item listener under `switchMap` over the parent stream rebuilds the WHOLE fan on every parent emission — key it by the parent-ID SET (`distinct`) so it only rebuilds on membership change; a `.take(n)` cap applied AFTER `.reversed` keeps the wrong end — cap on ([Malingisslen/butlery](https://github.com/Malingisslen/butlery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/firebase-backend-security.reads-cache-offline.knowledge.md) · ★1)
- **firebase-backend-security.repo-guards-audit.knowledge**: - Prefer a targeted `update({changed fields})` over `set(merge:true)` from a possibly-stale local base (offline/queued replay) — a merge re-sends unchanged privileged fields, letting the one caller allowed to touch them (the owner) resurrect a removed member via a stale write. - **firebase-backend-security.rules.knowledge**: A gate written as `!('f' in request.resource.data) || check(...)` is DEAD until a writer stamps `f` — grep the repository's hand-built map (not the model's `toFirestore()`, which a merge-writing method may bypass) before calling such a rule a control, and grade the rules test the same way: a fixture builder that unconditionally stamps `f` proves a payload production never sends, and goes green for ([Malingisslen/butlery](https://github.com/Malingisslen/butlery/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/firebase-backend-security.rules.knowledge.md) · ★1)

([gretjia/turingosv3](https://github.com/gretjia/turingosv3/blob/HEAD/.gemini/agents/fixer.md))
- **fixer**: Use this agent when a lead session hands one tracked issue to an implementer on an agent team and the change must go through the compound-engineering toolchain. Typical triggers include a lead spawning a teammate to fix one ready-for-agent bug, a batch of issues fanned out one fixer per issue, and a REWORK verdict sent back to the fixer that owns the branch. Not for reviewing work (use the verifier) and not for opening pull requests, which the lead owns. See "When to invoke" in the agent body for worked scenarios. ([Integral-Productivity/integral-productivity-engineering-claude-plugin](https://github.com/Integral-Productivity/integral-productivity-engineering-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer.md))
- **fixer**: Refactoring agent that analyzes review feedback and applies intelligent code improvements from auto-fix to deep remediation ([jaebit/claudemate](https://github.com/jaebit/claudemate/blob/HEAD/plugins/crew/agents/fixer.md))
- **fixer**: You fix what one round of review comments and one round of failing checks ask for, on a pull request someone else is babysitting. The orchestrator spawned you, commits your work, and answers the threads on your behalf. Your final message is its return value — data, not a message to a human. ([janbiehl/jbi-skills](https://github.com/janbiehl/jbi-skills/blob/HEAD/skills/babysit/agents/fixer.md))
- **fixer**: Sonnet debugging engineer. Use when something is broken and the cause is unknown — failing tests, runtime errors, CI failures. It reproduces, isolates the root cause, fixes it minimally, and proves the fix. Give it the failing command and the observed output. ([Kriangsak1416/ko-skills](https://github.com/Kriangsak1416/ko-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer.md))
- **fixer**: Uses the local model to rewrite bad or missing titles and meta descriptions within length limits, and builds a redirect map for broken links. The champion-tier value-add. ([Manish85-cell/seo-analyzer](https://github.com/Manish85-cell/seo-analyzer/blob/HEAD/seo-command-center/agents/fixer.md) · 6 copies)
- **fixer**: Fixer per edit non banali. Fix multi-file, refactor con dipendenze, o casi dove il piano è definito da chi coordina ma l'esecuzione richiede giudizio sull'impatto fuori scope. Non committa mai, non amplia lo scope. Per script brevi o edit singolo file con piano già completo usa fixer-quick. ([martino-vigiani/skills](https://github.com/martino-vigiani/skills/blob/HEAD/plugins/core-agents/agents/fixer.md))
- **fixer**: Fix sub-agent. Applies a validated fix list to a DISJOINT set of files (never shared with another fixer). Edits only — no git/build/test, no docs/tests. Spawn several in parallel, partitioned by file. ([meir7651231-ui/buildsmart](https://github.com/meir7651231-ui/buildsmart/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/fixer.md) · 2 copies)
- **fixer**: The fast lane for bugs and small changes that don't warrant a full slice. Dispatched by the orchestrator via /fix, off the behavior-first pipeline. Writes product code under backend/ or frontend/ (never tests or specs). Returns a four-status report. ([Muhanad-husn/AEO](https://github.com/Muhanad-husn/AEO/blob/HEAD/source/v1-archive/claude/agents/fixer.md))
- **fixer**: Applies a single, scoped code fix from a review finding. Edits only the files in the fix's footprint, adds or updates the covering test for behavior fixes, makes no unrelated changes, and reports back in one line. ([NebsterSK/claude-settings](https://github.com/NebsterSK/claude-settings/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer.md))
- **fixer**: Fast implementation specialist. Receives complete context and task spec, executes code changes efficiently. ([YanzuoLu/oh-my-claude-code-slim](https://github.com/YanzuoLu/oh-my-claude-code-slim/blob/HEAD/plugins/oh-my-claude-code-slim/agents/fixer.md))
- **fixer-a11y**: pragmatist a11y fixer — composed from the pragmatist personality x a11y domain x fixer phase via /guild-compile. ([krambuhl/agents](https://github.com/krambuhl/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild/agents/fixer-a11y.md))

- **fixer-bootflow**: Owns bootloader control flow. Fixes null_ret, console_silent and shell_exit_early through the entry redirect trampoline, console output routing and the getline timeout branch. Traces the caller before redirecting anything, and changes exactly one place per round. ([hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost](https://github.com/hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-bootflow.md))
- **fixer-css-architecture**: pragmatist css-architecture fixer — composed from the pragmatist personality x css-architecture domain x fixer phase via /guild-compile. ([krambuhl/agents](https://github.com/krambuhl/agents/blob/HEAD/plugins/guild/agents/fixer-css-architecture.md))
- **fixer-el3**: Owns EL3 and exception-level faults. Fixes smc_undef, psci_suspend, hvc_pkvm and fpu_trap by editing the SMC handler, the PSCI conduit and the CPU reset state. Disassembles ELR with capstone to confirm the instruction really is the cause before patching, and changes exactly one place per round. ([hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost](https://github.com/hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-el3.md))
- **fixer-general**: Last-resort fixer with no domain boundary. Invoked only after the specialist fixers have declined a stop point (not_mine or no_new_change), which means the fault has no owner yet. Unlike the specialists it may edit any machine source, rebuild, and run, so that a coherent multi-part mechanism can be treated in one round. In exchange it must record what it did in a form that lets a real specialist be written later. ([hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost](https://github.com/hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-general.md))
- **fixer-high**: Applies a supplied set of findings inside an isolated worktree, and nothing else. Runs at high effort — dispatch the variant the resolver names. ([Smiley73/tagteam](https://github.com/Smiley73/tagteam/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-high.md) · ★1)
- **fixer-kernel**: Owns kernel-side faults. Fixes security_gate, kernel_oops, gic_ppi and rootfs_mount through kernel .text patches with mandatory pre-image verification, GIC wiring corrections, DT fstab injection and dm-linear supermount. Applies a byte patch only when the original bytes match the expected value, and changes exactly one place per round. ([hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost](https://github.com/hyu-sslab/sboot-rehost/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-kernel.md))
- **fixer-low**: Applies a supplied set of findings inside an isolated worktree, and nothing else. Runs at low effort — dispatch the variant the resolver names. ([Smiley73/tagteam](https://github.com/Smiley73/tagteam/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-low.md) · ★1)
- **fixer-max**: Applies a supplied set of findings inside an isolated worktree, and nothing else. Runs at max effort — dispatch the variant the resolver names. ([Smiley73/tagteam](https://github.com/Smiley73/tagteam/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-max.md) · ★1)
- **fixer-medium**: Applies a supplied set of findings inside an isolated worktree, and nothing else. Runs at medium effort — dispatch the variant the resolver names. ([Smiley73/tagteam](https://github.com/Smiley73/tagteam/blob/HEAD/agents/fixer-medium.md) · ★1)
- **fixer-memory**: Owns the memory map and peripheral windows. Fixes data_abort_unmapped, infinite_poll and unmapped_mmio by editing MemoryRegion definitions and read callbacks in machine.c. - **fixer-secureboot**: Owns the bootloader's own verified boot. - **fixer-storage**: Owns the vendor storage controller (UFS HCI). Uses the real driver as the instrument, models only what it observed, and escalates to .ko disassembly when the log does not show it. Adaptive toggles are absolutely forbidden. - **flow-merger**: Use this agent to execute a merge node of a /flow DAG. Records validators.json with each step's pass/fail/skipped status. Composes superpowers:verification-before-completion. &lt;example&gt; &lt;context&gt; flow SKILL dispatches the merger for a merge node with two parents. &lt;/context&gt; &lt;user_request&gt;… ([0pg/cc-marketplace](https://github.com/0pg/cc-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/flow/agents/flow-merger.md))
